## Manual checks

- Done in the browser pane (Chrome 141, Windows) on the 0.5.0 build: en, de and ar at 375 px, 1024 px and 1280 px.
- First impression: the logo lockup (cut-corner mark plus wordmark) sits in the header and footer and matches the favicon; the hero splits seven/five at 1024 px and five/seven at 1280 px with the h1 at `text-6xl`; the proof strip shows three figures with hairlines (value above label, so "automatisierte Linien" and "99,98 %" wrap inside their column); the highlight card floats over the media's top-end corner at lg+ (top-start in Arabic) and sits under the media on phones; no dot grid, two glows.
- Header fit: at 1024 px the German and Arabic bars measure 1009 px scroll width in a 1009 px viewport (no overflow) with the nav at `px-2`, the CTA at `px-4` and the sound toggle hidden until xl; the German h1 wraps to four lines with no mid-word break.
- Motion by purpose: heading groups rise and their eyebrow rule draws from the start edge; grid items stagger; media rows slide; testimonial cards and the CTA panel settle from 96 %; logo-cloud wordmarks slide in from the start; the timeline rule draws down; the hero stays still.
- Count-up: with the pane fronted, scrolling the stats band into view was caught mid-count (176, 5 %, 3, 13.69 % on the way to 1,284, 38 %, 19, 99.98 %); the final text is the build-time string; a hidden pane throttles animation frames, which is why a background check sees only final values. Nothing counts in the hero.
- Two across: team and integration tiles sit two across at 375 px in en and ar (RTL mirrored) with `p-4`; the stats band holds "99.98%" at `text-4xl` on phones without wrapping; no page scrolls horizontally at 375 px (`a11y/reflow` covers 320 and 375 on every page).
- Sound: clicking the footer toggle sets `aria-pressed="true"` on all three toggles and `sessionStorage.sound = "1"`, swaps the icon to `volume-2`, survives navigation to another page, and clicking again turns it off; no console errors. The sounds themselves were not heard in this session (no audio output on the test machine); their synthesis runs only after the first click.
- Lighthouse not re-run in this session (five runs per page and profile take about an hour on 288 pages); the changes add no requests, no layout animation and one 2.2 KB script, so the 0.4.0 scores are expected to hold; a run on `/en/` and `/en/about/team/` is the follow-up.

- Gate runs: the full a11y sweep (288 pages, 264 s) passed on the build before the last change (band numerals `text-4xl` on phones); this report's a11y line is the sampled run (`--sample`, one page per page type and locale, 139 s) on the final build. Lighthouse skipped.
